Normally split DAM PSSE stations

A station is split in a snapshot when the in-service branches landing at that station (same stationName+kV in AW.dbo.ercotDampsseStation) touch more than one distinct PSSE bus number. Bus numbers are random per snapshot (they change even hour to hour), so all comparison is strictly within a single (awDateID, HE) snapshot; we sample HE=12 once per day over this summer only (user choice): awDateID 46173–46240 (2026-06-01 … 2026-08-07). Endpoint→station mapping honours fromToFlipped (ignoring it produces 4,844 phantom multi-bus stations vs 1,353 on the latest snapshot). Source: AW.dbo.ercotDampsseTimeseries ⋈ ercotDampsseDef, fileTypes 1 (lines) + 2 (transformers), inService=1. Read-only; nothing written to any database.

Why this exists: at a normally split station one side may serve no load, so a Closed line into it carries no flow — two Closed lines there are not a real parallel circuit. The cut-in / parallel-circuit topology check must consult this reference set (KINGFISHER and PLATEAUT false positives came from exactly this confusion).
Validation (known ground truth):
KINGFISHER (DAM KNGFSHER 345 kV, negative control (new station) - must NOT be normally_split): split in 1 of 41 snapshots (2.4%) → rarely_split
REYST (teid 4850 / 7090__A) (DAM REYST 69 kV, one of JKSES/REYST expect SPLIT): split in 67 of 68 snapshots (98.5%) → normally_split
JKSES (teid 4850 / 7090__A) (DAM JKSES 69 kV, one of JKSES/REYST expect SPLIT): split in 68 of 68 snapshots (100.0%) → normally_split
PLATEAUT (DAM PLATE 138 kV, negative control (new station) - must NOT be normally_split): split in 55 of 59 snapshots (93.2%) → normally_split_new_station
  • 1287 normally_split
  • 34 normally_split_new_station
  • 56 sometimes_split
  • 129 rarely_split
  • 5772 never_split (not listed)

normally_split (1287)
